    KEY: It inhibits the release of arachidonate from membrane phospholipids by phospholipase A2.  ||  wrong: It blocks eicosanoid receptors. / It destroys arachidonate in the blood. / It inhibits cyclooxygenase.
[11] Why are eicosanoids called paracrine hormones?
    KEY: They act only on cells near where they are made.  ||  wrong: They act only inside the cell that makes them. / They are carried in the blood to distant organs. / They are stored in the pancreas.
[12] How do steroid hormones differ from cholesterol?
    KEY: They lack the alkyl chain on ring D and are more polar.  ||  wrong: They are made from arachidonate. / They contain a fatty acid in ester linkage. / They have no ring system.
[13] Why do very low concentrations of a steroid hormone produce a response?
    KEY: Its receptor binds it with very high affinity.  ||  wrong: It dissolves the plasma membrane. / It is an enzyme and acts catalytically. / It is converted into ATP.
[14] What is the fundamental event in vision?
    KEY: Light converts 11-cis-retinal to all-trans-retinal in rhodopsin.  ||  wrong: Light cleaves β-carotene into two molecules of retinal. / Opsin is hydrolyzed to amino acids. / Retinoic acid binds a nuclear receptor.
[15] How does warfarin act as an anticoagulant?
    KEY: It inhibits the formation of active prothrombin, which needs vitamin K.  ||  wrong: It binds Ca2+ in the blood. / It inhibits cyclooxygenase in platelets. / It is an antioxidant.
[16] What do ubiquinone and plastoquinone have in common?
    KEY: Both are lipid-soluble isoprenoids that carry electrons, in mitochondria and chloroplasts respectively.  ||  wrong: Both are fat-soluble vitamins needed for clotting. / Both are polyketide antibiotics. / Both are steroid hormones.
[17] What is the job of dolichols?
    KEY: They anchor sugar units to the membrane and activate them for transfer.  ||  wrong: They absorb visible light in the retina. / They carry electrons in the respiratory chain. / They emulsify dietary fat.
[18] Why are carotenoids colored?
    KEY: Their long system of conjugated double bonds absorbs visible light.  ||  wrong: They are esterified to cholesterol. / They contain iron. / They have a charged phosphate group.
[19] Which of these is a polyketide?
    KEY: Erythromycin  ||  wrong: Cortisol / Prostaglandin E2 / Vitamin E
